# Nodus development log

This log records actual development and publication outcomes. Future runs read the newest entry together with [the roadmap](ROADMAP.md) and [the development loop](DEVELOPMENT_LOOP.md). Historical tests are not a claim that those checks ran again in a later documentation-only change.

## 2026-11-12 — ND-000: establish the continuous-development plan

**Request:** Hourly development across usability, capabilities, design, personalization and animation. New branches for special/large changes. Update the roadmap whenever a capability becomes available.

**Baseline inspected:** GitHub main `f0958145973a130a0041604cb08f36925fe19e26`; Site source `04db17cd56bf56f157c4371eaa05dd91a1c7e61a`; common tree `4a00c185218e8a627dfccf9c60ae32cc1b10b54b`. Site version 11 had a successful publication. The current application already includes multi-selection and personal-space deletion. Its previous engineering pass recorded 205 passing automated tests, TypeScript, a production build and the two linked browser QA reports.

**Plan added:** Twenty-two concrete roadmap items with acceptance criteria, dependencies and branch paths; explicit status distinction between work in progress, verification and actual release; a per-run workflow with guarded source synchronization, overlap lease, behavioral verification and persistent checkpoints. README links to the plan. Verification wording now links the completed selection/deletion browser report instead of leaving that pass described as pending.

**Product-code scope:** Documentation and automation setup only. No application feature is marked newly released by this entry; no historical tests are presented as newly executed. The first product slice is ND-001, honest search-result counts and full project paths, followed by focus mode and collapsible branches on separate feature branches.

**Automation record (confirmed by the native create result):** `توسعهٔ ساعتی Nodus`, ID `Automation_7d6aae4eda24819196a386dcb94e59d8`, enabled, `RRULE:FREQ=HOURLY;INTERVAL=1`, exact schedule. **Ownership control:** The separate `automation/nodus-control` branch was initialized with an idle lease at commit `13c255c2a0c999ba7937d2d36404fde4dfbe440e`. Cross-run state belongs to that branch, never the application source. ## 2026-11-12 — ND-001: honest search result counts and project paths (in progress)

**Run:** `nodus-auto-20261112-run01-nd001`; work branch `fix/nd-001-search-clarity`; base GitHub `26fafac05c320ed5db30a0ee908dd816714acdd9`, tree `cf93a13f4c7da2530b9ff07efba74daf5b20a618`. Site checkpoint source `eb8a9ad1f470447bf15634efbaa47531924901c5` (saved version 11+documentation deployment); live tree has not been freshly fetched in this run because Sites actions are not exposed here.

**Problem:** Search silently truncates to 70 while the meta reads `matches.length`, misrepresenting total hits, and shows only the immediate board name rather than nested project ancestry. The first 70 matching nodes are reachable but later hits cannot be opened from search.

**Intended slice:** Count all matching nodes with a bounded initial page of 70, provide an explicit next-results action, and display a complete root-to-board location per result. Preserve existing title/text/asset-name matching, search selection, keyboard handling, FA/EN labels, and graph persistence. No user document writes or schema changes.

**Acceptance:** 0/70/71/151+ matches report correct visible and total counts; further matches can be opened; deep nested and duplicate-titled boards display full location; Persian and English search behave as before; defensive malformed containment cannot loop. Run targeted logic tests, TypeScript, production build and real desktop/mobile app inspection before marking Verified or merging/publishing. A missing test or UI preview is a Blocked verification gate, not a pass.

**Checkpoint:** Implement in a pure helper plus search dialog integration; add regression tests. UI browser, full repository typecheck/build and Site release require a supported checkout and Site runtime and may remain blocked in this execution. Keep change on this branch until gates pass.

**Run 1 outcome (2026-11-12, branch only):** Search logic and dialog integration committed to `fix/nd-001-search-clarity`; current feature commit `e8c143d1e9ca756b33093fc919e8dfb22fb04d62`. Exact count beyond 70, Show more, complete project paths and wrapped narrow-layout labels implemented. Isolated tests **5 passed / 0 failed**, exact helper and test blob SHAs verified, isolated helper TS with stub types passed. Full app TypeScript, full tests, production build, browser screenshots and Sites source/deployment were **not available**; no release or GitHub main merge. [QA detail](ND-001-SEARCH-QA.md). Next: restore supported runtime, validate, reconcile live source and release only after gates pass.


**Run 2 scope (ND-001, 2026-11-12 18:20 UTC):** Continue `fix/nd-001-search-clarity` from GitHub feature head `c7e28788650e67c2d9c327817160ad8cced15f23`, tree `85f09c3e2a77054016943b4d2bd8681784d640ab`; main stays `26fafac05c320ed5db30a0ee908dd816714acdd9`. The last run's 70-at-a-time **cumulative** Show more leaves all earlier result buttons mounted; with the 5,000-node limit a repeated search can eventually render thousands of buttons. This run will use **bounded forward/backward pages** (at most 70 rendered results) while keeping exact global totals and full project paths; prevent page drift after query or document changes. Acceptance: 151 matches produce 70/70/11 with stable non-overlapping IDs, out-of-range offset clamps safely, query edits reset page, and keyboard-accessible FA/EN previous/next controls do not mutate document data. Scope: search helper, search dialog and targeted tests/docs; no data model or Site access changes. Repository TypeScript/build, desktop/390px screenshots and GitHub/Sites reconciliation are still required before Verified or Released. No other feature work starts while this one remains open.

Actual counts and full nested project paths remain, with a visible range (e.g. 71–140 of 151). The results list resets scroll to top when paging and resets offset when search text, search dialog or launch context changes. When the document shrinks, a stale page offset recovers to the last non-empty page. No schema, shared operations or saved graph changes. ## 2026-11-12 — ND-001 manual run: better Persian phrase and project-path discovery (scope)

Run ID: `nodus-manual-20261112T221119Z`. Continue existing `fix/nd-001-search-clarity` from `d182c255fbf371ce8a688e870a26a4681dd43ed5`, tree `8abd388dd7a0520355646aab3564b0eee66189b7`; GitHub main observed `26fafac05c320ed5db30a0ee908dd816714acdd9`. Site source last checkpoint `eb8a9ad1f470447bf15634efbaa47531924901c5`, **not independently accessible or reconfirmed in this run**. Exclusive run lease acquired on control branch with guarded ref update.

Problem: Persian users commonly alternate between ordinary spaces, joiners and no spaces in compound words (e.g. «کتاب‌های», «کتاب های», «کتابهای»). The current normalization ignores ZWNJ but not ordinary whitespace, so titles and project paths that exist can still be hard to locate. Nested paths contain visible slash separators. No graph or stored content should be rewritten.

This run's bounded change: add a comparison-only, field-local compact fallback to `lib/thought-search.ts` for titles/notes/attachment filenames and a path fallback that recognizes root/nested separators. Preserve exact matches, case/Arabic-character handling, result order, count, 70-result paging, navigation IDs, and existing persisted documents. Avoid concatenating fields for the new compact fallback. Add targeted regression tests; run isolated Node tests and helper TypeScript if available.

Acceptance: spaced and unspaced Persian compounds match in either direction; parent/child project paths can be typed with or without slash separators; unrelated fields must not be merged into a false match; current 151-result pagination and previous Unicode tests stay green. No UI control, schema, shared operation or auth change. Project-wide tests/build, real 390px/desktop browser UI and authenticated Site source/deployment comparison remain release gates. If unavailable, keep ND-001 **In progress on branch only** and record Blocked checks rather than ship.

## Run 7 scope — unordered keywords for locating thoughts (2026-11-13T02:21:40.180Z)

**Roadmap:** ND-001, In progress; continue `fix/nd-001-search-clarity`. GitHub main `26fafac05c320ed5db30a0ee908dd816714acdd9`, tree `cf93a13f4c7da2530b9ff07efba74daf5b20a618`; feature baseline `e5aad2f70e9ab75bac33781ff1cdccc8abbee53a`, tree `5a8525f8d7d41c39ab96485e57f43aa650528569`. Site tree reconciliation is still pending (last checkpoint source `eb8a9ad1f470447bf15634efbaa47531924901c5`; last known main-matching tree `cf93a13f4c7da2530b9ff07efba74daf5b20a618`).

**Observed problem:** In a crowded or irregular mind map, multi-word queries only match contiguous wording, so a reversed word order or keywords split across a title/note/project location can hide the relevant thought. This is a comparison-only findability enhancement, not a graph layout or persistence change.

**This bounded slice:** Append a lower-priority, order-independent **multi-keyword** fallback to `searchThoughts` after all existing title/path/asset/note relevance tiers. Require **every** normalized nonempty query word to occur in at least one searchable field of the **same** thought (title, notes, attachment name, or authorized current-workspace board path). Keep existing contiguous results ranked first, stable ties, 70-page results/total, Unicode normalization, no mutation, no extra graph writes, and no cross-room search. Do not change Enter/Tab/IME handlers, canvas positions, or port 4173.

**Acceptance checks:** 1) reverse-word-order and split-field Persian/English queries find the expected items; 2) missing keyword does not match; 3) deterministic relevance and 151-item paging still work; 4) graph and paths are unmodified; 5) exact-source regression tests and helper TypeScript pass. Full app tests/build/real desktop + 390px browser/Sites deployment remain separate mandatory release gates. **Checkpoint if unavailable:** keep branch only and mark release Blocked; no main/Site publish.

### 2026-11-13 23:18 UTC — ND-001 malformed adjacent percent escapes in saved link search (scope before code)

**Issue:** An imported saved URL may contain contiguous percent escapes where one invalid UTF-8 byte sits between otherwise valid percent-encoded Persian characters, e.g. `%D8%B3%FF%D8%A7`. The existing tolerant fallback decodes entire contiguous groups atomically; an invalid middle byte therefore hides *both valid characters* from search. This is a search-only defect, not a malformed URL repair request.

**Acceptance:** Preserve raw URL bytes and graph/revision/asset data. During derived search comparison, decode valid 1–4-byte UTF-8 percent sequences on either side of a malformed byte within the *same contiguous escape group*; keep invalid escape bytes verbatim. Preserve existing percent-decoded complete URL, query `+` behavior and ranking/page counts. Run red/green tests for Persian and Latin ASCII valid-invalid-valid sequences, incomplete sequences and ordinary well-formed URLs; review bounded complexity and helper TypeScript. Do not claim actual Nodus UI, complete test suite, current Site tree or publication without direct evidence.

### 2026-11-13 23:25 UTC — ND-001 malformed contiguous UTF-8 URL escapes (implementation + QA, branch only)

**Issue and change:** A malformed byte `%FF` in a contiguous URL escape run such as `%D8%B3%FF%D8%A7` previously prevented decoding *both valid Persian characters* even for local search comparison. ## 2026-11-14 00:17 UTC — ND-001 Persian numeric notation in search (scope before implementation)

Run: `nodus-nd001-20261114T0015Z-hourly-12`, guarded lease on `automation/nodus-control`. Source branch: `fix/nd-001-search-clarity` at `dbc4b2e262fe8e5437e8653aac9b0d2ada34839b`, tree `ced9e7c30cd693f1faa302b2a332e7a1be18d34c`. Main `26fafac05c320ed5db30a0ee908dd816714acdd9`; main tree `cf93a13f4c7da2530b9ff07efba74daf5b20a618`. Site remains a historical checkpoint only; no current Site tree / deployment verified, no authorized Sites skill available in this environment.

**User-visible problem:** Persian/Arabic digits already match ASCII digits, but the corresponding decimal separator `٫` (U+066B), thousands separator `٬` (U+066C), and percent sign `٪` (U+066A) are not comparison-equivalent to `.`, `,`, and `%`. Thus common shot numbers, dates, statistics, titles or filenames can be missed despite identical numeric meaning.

**Bounded slice / acceptance:** Normalize these **three** numeric marks in search-only strings, in both search-query and content directions (including project paths), without changing persisted text, URLs, schema, result ordering, 70/page behavior or Enter/Tab/IME. Add native regression tests for all three, quoted exact phrase and mixed Persian/ASCII numbers; preserve literal strings and cross-field guards. Touch only `lib/thought-search.ts` and a focused test file, then QA/roadmap/log. No visible UI change or animation. Run native focused tests and helper TypeScript if possible; otherwise record the exact isolated check performed. Full repo native tests/TS5.9.3/build, real FA/EN desktop+390px browser screenshots at 4173, authenticated two-account collaboration, and current Sites head/tree remain release gates. This work stays **In progress on the feature branch** until all relevant gates pass; no main merge or Site publication.

## 2026-11-16 — ND-001 search-result explanation parity (scope before code)

Run `nodus-auto-20261116T192005-nd001-gates`; held guarded lease on `automation/nodus-control` at `96ffeeac525ba608b91c95e726f30a2b65da3995`. **Concrete problem:** ND-001's recently added search keyword tokenizer removes surrounding straight/curly quotes from separately quoted terms, but `searchResultPreview` still splits the raw normalized query using whitespace. Consequently a real search hit for `‘Forest’ ‘Lighting’` across a note, comment, URL or project path can have a missing/wrong source explanation or an excerpt pointing to the beginning rather than the actual matched text. In multi-field matching this hides why the result was found, especially on narrow screens.

**Bounded slice:** Reuse the exact read-only `searchKeywords` tokenizer in `lib/search-result-preview.ts`, add regression cases to `tests/search-result-preview.test.ts` and record focused QA. Do not alter `searchThoughts`, ranking, 70-result paging, source text, IDs, permissions, graph state, file storage, React keyboard/IME, or Site deployment.

**Acceptance:** Two independently quoted terms across one note show a relevant note/excerpt; two terms across different fields show honest `multiple`; signed URL-only hits retain host-only `link` explanation; annotation-only hit shows `comment`; full balanced phrases remain exact/field-local; frozen source data unchanged. Attempt native focused tests + TS and full native suite/build only if available; real FA/EN desktop and 390px browser screenshot, persistence and authenticated Sites tree equality are release gates.
